|\acute{l}|+|\hat{l}|\leq\mathcal{M}+2,|\hat{l}|\leq 2\end{subarray}}\mathcal{R}_{k\acute{l}\hat{l}},

and

\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}=\Pi_{\eta}\setminus{\mathcal{R}},

where
\Pi_{\eta}
is obtained by Theorem
2.6
. Then it follows that for each
\xi\in\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}
, the frequencies
\breve{\omega}
and
\breve{\Omega}(x;\xi)
are
(\acute{\eta},\mathcal{N},\mathcal{M})
-non-resonant.

Theorem 4.1

(Partial normal form of order
\mathcal{M}+2
)
.

Consider the normal form of order
2

\breve{H}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\breve{N}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+\breve{P}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

obtained in Theorem
2.6
. Given any positive integer
\mathcal{M}
and
0<\acute{\eta}<1
, there exists
a small
\rho_{0}>0
and
depending on
s_{0},r_{0},n
and
\mathcal{M}
. For each
0<\rho<\rho_{0}
and any integer
\mathcal{N}
satisfying

\mathcal{N}_{0}<\mathcal{N}<(\frac{\acute{\eta}}{2\rho})^{1/2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}},

(where
C_{0}
is a constant depending on
n
and
\tau
) and for each
\xi\in\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}
, there is a symplectic map

\Phi:D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)\rightarrow D(5\varrho,5\rho,5\rho),

(where
\varrho=\frac{\mathcal{M}}{\mathcal{N}^{2}}
) such that

\displaystyle\breve{\breve{H}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\breve{H}\circ\Phi=

\displaystyle N(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+Z(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)

\displaystyle+Q(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+T(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)

(4.2)

where

N(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\breve{\omega}_{j}(\xi)y_{j}+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}_{*}}\frac{\breve{\Omega}_{j}(x;\xi)}{j}z_{j}\bar{z}_{j},

Z(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{4\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2}Z_{j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

Q(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2}Q_{j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

and

T(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{j\geq 3}T_{j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

(4.3)

with

Z_{j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{2|\alpha|+2|\beta|+2|\mu|=j,|\mu|=0}Z^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\beta}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\mu},

+\sum_{2|\alpha|+2|\beta|+2|\mu|=j,|\mu|=1}Z^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\beta}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\mu},\quad 4\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2,

Q_{j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j,|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3}Q^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},\quad 3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2,

T_{j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j}T^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},\quad j\geq\mathcal{M}+3,

and

T_{j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j\\
\beta\neq\gamma,\mu\neq\nu,|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2\end{subarray}}T^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},\quad 3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2.

Moreover, the following estimates hold:

(1) the symplectic map
\Psi
satisfies

\|\Psi-id\|_{p,D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)}\leq\frac{c\rho\mathcal{N}^{C}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}},

(4.4)

\|D\Psi-Id\|_{p,D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)}\leq\frac{c\mathcal{N}^{C}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}},

where
C
is a constant depending on
n
and
\tau
.

(2) The functions
{Z}
,
Q
and
T
satisfy

|||Q|||_{D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq c\rho^{3},

\|X_{Z}\|_{p-1,D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq c\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right),

and

\|X_{T}\|_{p-1,D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq c\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{\mathcal{M}},

(4.5)

where
c
is a constant depending on
s_{0},r_{0},n
, and
\mathcal{M}
.

Remark 4.2
.

From (
4.3
), it is easy to see that the function
T
mainly consists of two parts. One part has the monomials of the form
T^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu}
with
2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|\geq\mathcal{M}+3
, we denote it by
\mathcal{A}_{1}
. Another part contains the monomials of the form
T^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu}
with
3\leq 2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|\leq\mathcal{M}+2
, we denote it by
\mathcal{A}_{2}
. In addition, the norm of the part
\mathcal{A}_{1}
and
\mathcal{A}_{2}
has the estimate (
4.5
). It means that
T
is small enough for the long time stability estimate.

4.1
Iterative Lemma

Set

\varrho^{\prime}=\frac{\varrho}{12\mathcal{M}}:=\frac{1}{12\mathcal{N}^{2}},\quad\rho^{\prime}=\frac{\rho}{12\mathcal{M}}.

Let
2\leq j_{0}\leq\mathcal{M}+2
and denote

D_{j_{0}}=D(5\varrho-2(j_{0}-2)\varrho^{\prime},5\rho-2(j_{0}-2)\rho^{\prime},5\rho-2(j_{0}-2)\rho^{\prime}),

D^{\prime}_{j_{0}}=D(5\varrho-(2(j_{0}-2)+1)\varrho^{\prime},5\rho-(2(j_{0}-2)+1)\rho^{\prime},5\rho-(2(j_{0}-2)+1)\rho^{\prime}),

D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}=D(5\varrho-(2(j_{0}-2)+2)\varrho^{\prime},5\rho-(2(j_{0}-2)+2)\rho^{\prime},5\rho-(2(j_{0}-2)+2)\rho^{\prime}),

and

D_{j_{0}+1}\subset D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\subset D^{\prime}_{j_{0}}\subset D_{j_{0}}.

Lemma 4.3
.

Consider the partial normal form of order
j_{0}\ (2\leq j_{0}\leq\mathcal{M}+1)

\begin{split}H_{j_{0}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=&\breve{N}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+Z_{j_{0}}(y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+R_{j_{0}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)\\
&+Q_{j_{0}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+T_{j_{0}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),\end{split}

where

\displaystyle\breve{N}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\breve{\omega}_{j}(\xi)y_{j}+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}_{*}}\frac{\breve{\Omega}_{j}(x;\xi)}{j}z_{j}\bar{z}_{j},

\displaystyle Z_{j_{0}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{3\leq j\leq j_{0}}Z_{j_{0}j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

(4.6)

\displaystyle R_{j_{0}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{j_{0}+1\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2}R_{j_{0}j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

\displaystyle Q_{j_{0}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2}Q_{j_{0}j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)

\displaystyle T_{j_{0}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{j\geq 3}T_{j_{0}j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

(4.7)

with

\begin{split}Z_{j_{0}j}(y,z,\bar{z};\xi)&=\sum_{2|\alpha|+2|\beta|+2|\mu|=j,|\mu|=0}Z_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\beta}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\mu}\\
&=\sum_{2|\alpha|+2|\beta|+2|\mu|=j,|\mu|=1}Z_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\beta}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\mu},\end{split}

R_{j_{0}j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j,|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2}R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

Q_{j_{0}j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j,|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3}Q_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

T_{j_{0}j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j}T_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},\quad j\geq\mathcal{M}+3,

and

T_{j_{0}j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j\\
\beta\neq\gamma,\mu\neq\nu,|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2\end{subarray}}T_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},\quad 3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2.

Suppose
Z_{j_{0}j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)
,
R_{j_{0}j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)
and
Q_{j_{0}j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)
satisfy the following estimates

\displaystyle\|X_{Z_{j_{0}j}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}},\ \frac{1}{\rho^{2}}|||{Z_{j_{0}j}}|||_{D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+4)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3},

(4.8)

\displaystyle\|X_{R_{j_{0}j}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}},\ \frac{1}{\rho^{2}}|||{R_{j_{0}j}}|||_{D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3},

(4.9)

\displaystyle\|X_{Q_{j_{0}j}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}},\ \frac{1}{\rho^{2}}|||{Q_{j_{0}j}}|||_{D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3},

(4.10)

where
C_{0}>40(\tau+n)
is a constant depending on
n
and
\tau
.
The function
T_{j_{0}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)
is already small enough for the long time stability estimate

\|X_{T_{j_{0}}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{\mathcal{M}}.

(4.11)

Then there exists a symplectic map
\Psi_{j_{0}}:D_{j_{0}+1}\rightarrow D_{j_{0}}
satisfying the estimate

\|\Psi_{j_{0}}-id\|_{p,D_{j_{0}}^{\prime}}\lessdot\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-1},

(4.12)

||D\Psi_{j_{0}}-Id||_{p,D_{j_{0}+1}}\lessdot\rho^{-1}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-1},

(4.13)

such that

\begin{split}\breve{\breve{H}}_{j_{0}+1}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=&H_{j_{0}}\circ\Psi_{j_{0}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\breve{N}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+Z_{j_{0}+1}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)\\
&+R_{j_{0}+1}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+Q_{j_{0}+1}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+T_{j_{0}+1}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),\end{split}

where
Z_{j_{0}+1},R_{j_{0}+1}
,
Q_{j_{0}+1}
and
T_{j_{0}+1}
satisfy (
4.8
), (
4.4
),(
4.10
) and (
4.11
) by replacing
j_{0}
by
j_{0}+1
there.

4.2
The derivation of homological equations

Let

F_{j_{0}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j_{0}+1,|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2}F_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

and
\Psi_{j_{0}}=X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}|_{t=1}
be the time-
1
-map of the Hamiltonian vector field
X_{F_{j_{0}}}
.

According to Taylor’s formula, we have

\displaystyle H_{j_{0}+1}=

\displaystyle H_{j_{0}}\circ\Psi_{j_{0}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\breve{N}+Z_{j_{0}}+R_{j_{0}}+Q_{j_{0}}+T_{j_{0}})\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}|_{t=1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\breve{N}+\{\breve{N},F_{j_{0}}\}+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\{\{\breve{N},F_{j_{0}}\},F_{j_{0}}\}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}dt

\displaystyle+R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+\int_{0}^{1}\{R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)},F_{j_{0}}\}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}dt

\displaystyle+(Z_{j_{0}}+R_{j_{0}}-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Q_{j_{0}}+T_{j_{0}})\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}|_{t=1}.

We need to solve the homological equation

\{\breve{N},F_{j_{0}}\}+R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}=\widehat{Z_{j_{0}}}+\widehat{T_{j_{0}}},

(4.14)

where

\widehat{T_{j_{0}}}=\{\breve{N},(1-\Gamma_{K})G_{j_{0}}\},

(4.15)

\displaystyle\widehat{Z_{j_{0}}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{2|\alpha|+2|\beta|+2|\mu|=j_{0}+1,|\mu|=0}R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(0;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\beta}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\mu}

(4.16)

\displaystyle+\sum_{2|\alpha|+2|\beta|+2|\mu|=j_{0}+1,|\mu|=1}R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\beta}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\mu},

(4.17)

with
R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(0;\xi)
being the
0
-th Fourier series of
R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(x;\xi)
and
G_{j_{0}}
being the solution of equation (
4.14
) without the term
\widehat{T_{j_{0}}}
.
After solving the homological equation (
4.14
), we have

H_{j_{0}+1}=\breve{N}+Z_{j_{0}+1}+R_{j_{0}+1}+Q_{j_{0}+1}+T_{j_{0}+1},

with

Z_{j_{0}+1}=Z_{j_{0}}+\widehat{Z_{j_{0}}},

(4.18)

and

\displaystyle R_{j_{0}+1}+Q_{j_{0}+1}+T_{j_{0}+1}

(4.19)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widehat{T_{j_{0}}}+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\{\{\breve{N},F_{j_{0}}\},F_{j_{0}}\}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\{R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Z_{j_{0}},F_{j_{0}}\}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}dt

(4.20)

\displaystyle+(R_{j_{0}}-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Q_{j_{0}}+T_{j_{0}})\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}|_{t=1}.

4.3
The solution of homological equation

The following lemma plays a key role in solving the homological equation.

Lemma 4.4
.

(
[
28
]
)
Consider the first-order partial differential equation (
4.14
)

{\bf i}\partial_{\omega}u+\lambda u+\mu(\phi)u=p(\phi),\quad\phi\in D(s),\lambda\in\mathbb{C},

(4.21)

for the unknown function
u
defined on the torus
D(s)
. Assume the following conditions hold.

(i) There are constants
\eta_{0},\eta>0
and
\tau>n
such that

\displaystyle|\langle k,\omega\rangle|\geq\frac{\eta_{0}}{|k|^{\tau}},\quad\forall k\in\mathbb{Z}^{n}\setminus\{0\},

\displaystyle|\langle k,\omega\rangle+\lambda|\geq\frac{\eta\gamma}{1+|k|^{\tau}},\quad\forall k\in\mathbb{Z}^{n}.

(4.22)

(ii)
\mu(\phi):D(s)\rightarrow\mathbb{C}
is real analytic and zero average,
\int_{\mathbb{T}^{n}}\mu(\phi)d\phi=0.
Moreover, assume

|\mu|_{D(s),\tau+1}:=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}^{n}}|\hat{\mu}_{k}||k|^{\tau+1}e^{|k|s}\leq C\alpha,

(4.23)

where
\hat{\mu}_{k}
is the
k
-Fourier coefficient of
\mu
.

(iii)
p
is real analytic in
D(s)
.

Then homological equation (
4.21
) has a unique solution
u(\phi)
defined in a narrower domain
D(s-\sigma)
with
0<\sigma<s
, which satisfies

\|u\|_{D(s-\sigma)}\leq\frac{c(n,\tau)}{\eta\gamma\sigma^{n+\tau}}e^{2C\alpha s/\eta_{0}}\|p\|_{D(s)}.

(4.24)

Remark 4.5
.

The present lemma is a variant version of Liu-Yuan’s result (Theorem 1.4 of
[
28
]
). In
[
28
]
, the parameter
\alpha
in both (
4.23
) and (
4.24
) is restricted to
\alpha=\gamma
where the parameter
\gamma
is that in the small divisor conditions (
4.22
). By checking the proof of Theorem 1.4 of
[
28
]
, we find that the restrict condition
\alpha=\gamma
can be dropped.

Now, we return to the homological equation (
4.14
). Writing
F_{j_{0}}=\Gamma_{K}G_{j_{0}}
, by (
4.15
), equation (
4.14
) becomes

\{\breve{N},G_{j_{0}}\}+R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}=\widehat{Z_{j_{0}}}.

Comparing the coefficients, equation (
4.14
) reads

(\partial_{\omega}+{\bf i}\langle\beta-\gamma,\acute{\Omega}\rangle+{\bf i}\langle\mu-\nu,\hat{\Omega}\rangle)G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}=R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu},

(4.25)

where
2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j_{0}+1\leq\mathcal{M}+2,|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2
.

In order to solve this homological equation, we split the term
\langle\mu-\nu,[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle
into the following three cases:

Case 1
:
|\mu-\nu|=1
and
|\langle\mu-\nu,[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|=|[\breve{\Omega}_{i}]|
, for some
i\geq\mathcal{N}+1.

Then, by assumption
A
and (
2.8
), there exists some constant
c_{1}>0
such that

|\langle\mu-\nu,[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|\geq\frac{c_{1}}{2}i^{2}\geq\frac{c_{1}}{2}i,

for some constant
c_{1}>0
.

Case 2
:
|\mu-\nu|=2
and
|\langle\mu-\nu,[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|=|[\breve{\Omega}_{i}]+[\breve{\Omega}_{j}]|
or
|\langle\mu-\nu,[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|=|[\breve{\Omega}_{i}]-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}]|
, (
i\neq\pm j
) for some
i,j\geq\mathcal{N}+1.

Then, we obtain

|\langle\mu-\nu,[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|\geq c_{1}(i^{2}\pm j^{2})-(i+j)c\eta^{6}\epsilon\geq\frac{c_{1}}{2}|i+j||i-j|\geq\frac{c_{1}}{2}\max\{|i|,|j|\}.

In Cases 1 and 2, we assume
|\acute{\Omega}_{j}(x,\xi)|\leq c_{2}|j|^{2}\leq c_{2}\mathcal{N}^{2}
. Thus, if

\max\{|i|,|j|\}\geq\frac{8}{c_{1}}c_{2}(\mathcal{M}+2)\mathcal{N}^{2},

then

\displaystyle|\langle\beta-\gamma,[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle+\langle\mu-\nu,[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|\geq

\displaystyle|\langle\mu-\nu,[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|-|\langle\beta-\gamma,[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{1}}{4}\max\{|i|,|j|\}+2c_{2}(\mathcal{M}+2)\mathcal{N}^{2}

\displaystyle-(1+c\eta^{6}\varepsilon)\cdot c_{2}|\acute{l}|\mathcal{N}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{1}}{4}\max\{|i|,|j|\}.

Thus, in cases 1 and 2, we have the following two subcases:

Subcase 1
: If
\max\{|i|,|j|\}\geq\frac{8}{c_{1}}c_{2}(\mathcal{M}+2)\mathcal{N}^{2}
, then we have that, for
(\mathcal{M}+2)c\eta^{6}\leq\varepsilon^{-1/2}
,

\|a(x)\|_{D(s_{0}/2)}:=\|\lambda^{-1}(\langle\beta-\gamma,\acute{\Omega}\rangle+\langle\mu-\nu,\hat{\Omega}\rangle-\lambda)\|_{D(s_{0}/2)}\leq(\mathcal{M}+2)c\eta^{6}\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon^{1/2},

where
\lambda:=\langle\beta-\gamma,[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle+\langle\mu-\nu,[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle
. Thus, equation (
4.25
) can be written as

{\bf i}\partial_{\omega}G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}+\lambda(1+a(x))G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}=R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}.

By Lemma
3.2
, one gets

|G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}|_{D^{\prime}_{j_{0}}}\leq\frac{\mathcal{N}^{(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}M_{\beta-\gamma,\mu-\nu}(\varrho^{\prime})^{20(\tau+n)}}\|R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D_{j_{0}}}

\leq\frac{\mathcal{N}^{(j_{0}+5)^{2}+20(\tau+n)}}{\acute{\eta}M_{\beta-\gamma,\mu-\nu}}\|R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D_{j_{0}}}.

(4.26)

In order to obtain the estimate for
\partial_{\xi}F_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}
, by taking the partial derivative with respect to
\xi
in both sides of the homological equation (
4.25
), we have

\displaystyle(\partial_{\omega}+{\bf i}\langle\beta-\gamma,\acute{\Omega}\rangle+{\bf i}\langle\mu-\nu,\hat{\Omega}\rangle)\partial_{\xi}G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi}R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}-\partial_{\xi}\omega\cdot\partial_{x}G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}-({\bf i}\langle\beta-\gamma,\partial_{\xi}\acute{\Omega}\rangle+{\bf i}\langle\mu-\nu,\partial_{\xi}\hat{\Omega}\rangle)\cdot G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}.

Similar to (
4.26
), one can obtain

|\partial_{\xi}G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}|_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}}\lessdot\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2(j_{0}+5)^{2}+40(\tau+n)}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}M_{\beta-\gamma,\mu-\nu}}\|R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D_{j_{0}}}.

(4.27)

From (
4.26
) and (
4.27
), one immediately obtains

\|G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2(j_{0}+5)^{2}+40(\tau+n)}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}M_{\beta-\gamma,\mu-\nu}}\|R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}.

Since
F^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}_{j_{0}}=\Gamma_{K}G^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}_{j_{0}}
, we also have

\|F_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2(j_{0}+5)^{2}+40(\tau+n)}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}M_{\beta-\gamma,\mu-\nu}}\|R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}.

(4.28)

Subcase 2
: If
\max\{|i|,|j|\}<\frac{8}{c_{1}}c_{2}(\mathcal{M}+2)\mathcal{N}^{2}
, we can use Lemma
4.4
to solve equation (
4.25
). Similar to subcase 1, we let
F^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}_{j_{0}}=\Gamma_{K}G^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}_{j_{0}}
. Letting

\lambda={\bf i}\langle\beta-\gamma,[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle+{\bf i}\langle\mu-\nu,[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle,\quad\mu={\bf i}\langle\beta-\gamma,\acute{\Omega}-[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle+{\bf i}\langle\mu-\nu,\hat{\Omega}-[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle,

u=G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}
and
p=R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}
in Lemma
4.4
, we obtain

\|\mu\|_{D(s_{j_{0}}),\tau+1}\leq\|\mu\|_{D(s_{0}/2),\tau+1}\leq C\mathcal{M}\mathcal{N}^{2}\varepsilon.

It immediately follows that

\displaystyle|G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}|_{D_{j_{0}}^{\prime}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{N}^{(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}M_{\beta-\gamma,\mu-\nu}(\varrho^{\prime})^{\tau+n}}e^{C\mathcal{M}\mathcal{N}^{2}\varepsilon s_{j_{0}}/\eta}\|R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{N}^{(j_{0}+5)^{2}+20(\tau+n)}}{\acute{\eta}M_{\beta-\gamma,\mu-\nu}}\|R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}},

since
C\mathcal{M}\mathcal{N}^{2}\varepsilon s_{j_{0}}/\eta\lessdot 1
.
Similar to subcase 1, one can obtain the estimate for
\partial_{\xi}G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}
and prove (
4.28
).

Case 3
. Let
|\mu-\nu|=2
and
\langle\mu-\nu,[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle=[\hat{\Omega}]_{i}-[\hat{\Omega}]_{j}
with
i=-j
.

For
i=-j
, by the momentum conservation for
\breve{\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}]=\sum_{k\neq 0}\breve{\Omega}_{j}(k;\xi)e^{{\bf i}\langle k,x\rangle}
, we have

-\sum_{b=1}^{n}j_{b}k_{b}-2j+\sum_{i}i(\beta_{i}-\gamma_{i})=0.

It implies that

|j|\leq C_{J_{n}}|k|+\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{M}\mathcal{N}.

Therefore,

|\breve{\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}]|_{D(s_{0}/2),\tau+1}=\sum_{0\neq k\in\mathbb{Z}^{n}}|\breve{\Omega}_{j}(k;\xi)||k|^{\tau+1}e^{|k|s_{0}/2}

\leq C\sum_{0\neq k\in\mathbb{Z}^{n}}|j^{-1}\breve{\Omega}_{j}(k;\xi)||k|^{\tau+1}(|k|+\mathcal{M}\mathcal{N})e^{|k|s_{0}/2}\leq C\epsilon\mathcal{M}\mathcal{N}.

Then, the homological equation (
4.25
) can be solved by the argument similar to subcase 2. In addition, the solution has the estimates similar to (
4.28
).

Case 4
.
|\mu-\nu|=0
. For this case, we can solve the homological equation (
4.25
) as in subcase 2, and obtain the similar estimate for its solution. Since
|\mu-\nu|=0
, the homological equation becomes

(\partial_{\omega}+{\bf i}\langle\beta-\gamma,\acute{\Omega}\rangle)G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}=R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}.

Setting
\lambda={\bf i}\langle\beta-\gamma,[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle
,
\mu={\bf i}\langle\beta-\gamma,\acute{\Omega}-[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle
,
u=G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}
and
p=R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}
in Lemma
4.4
, we obtain
\|\mu\|_{D(s_{j_{0}}),\tau+1}\leq\|\mu\|_{D(s_{0}/2),\tau+1}\leq C\mathcal{M}\mathcal{N}\varepsilon
. It follows that

\displaystyle|G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}|_{D_{j_{0}}^{\prime}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{N}^{(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}M_{\beta-\gamma,\mu-\nu}(\varrho^{\prime})^{\tau+n}}e^{C\mathcal{M}\mathcal{N}\varepsilon s_{j_{0}}/\eta}\|R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{N}^{(j_{0}+5)^{2}+20(\tau+n)}}{\acute{\eta}M_{\beta-\gamma,\mu-\nu}}\|R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}},

since
C\mathcal{M}\mathcal{N}\varepsilon s_{j_{0}}/\eta\lessdot 1
.
Similar to subcase 1, one can obtain the estimate for
\partial_{\xi}G_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}
and prove (
4.28
).

Combining
Cases 1, 2, 3
and
4
together,
F_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}
has the estimate

\|F_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2(j_{0}+5)^{2}+40(\tau+n)}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}M_{\beta-\gamma,\mu-\nu}}\|R_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}\|_{D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}

for all
2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j_{0}+1\leq\mathcal{M}+2,|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2
.

Therefore, from (
4.4
), we have

\displaystyle|||F_{j_{0}}|||^{*}_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2(j_{0}+5)^{2}+40(\tau+n)}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}|||R_{j_{0}}|||_{D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2(j_{0}+5)^{2}+40(\tau+n)}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\cdot\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho^{2}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-1},

(4.29)

and

\displaystyle\|X_{F_{j_{0}}}\|_{p,D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2(j_{0}+5)^{2}+40(\tau+n)}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\|X_{R_{j_{0}}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2(j_{0}+5)^{2}+40(\tau+n)}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\cdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-1},

(4.30)

which implies the estimates (
4.12
) and (
4.13
) in the iterative lemma.

4.4
Estimate
Z_{j_{0}+1},R_{j_{0}+1},Q_{j_{0}+1}
and
T_{j_{0}+1}

We rewrite
Z_{j_{0}+1}
as

Z_{j_{0}+1}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{3\leq j\leq j_{0}+1}Z_{(j_{0}+1)j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

where

Z_{(j_{0}+1)j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{|\alpha|+2|\beta|+2|\mu|=j,|\mu|\leq 1}Z_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\beta}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\mu}.

Then from (
4.6
),(
4.16
), (
4.17
) and (
4.18
), one gets

Z_{(j_{0}+1)j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=Z_{j_{0}j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),\quad 3\leq j\leq j_{0},

and

Z_{(j_{0}+1)(j_{0}+1)}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\widehat{Z_{j_{0}}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi).

Thus, for
3\leq j\leq j_{0}
, by iterative assumption (
4.8
), we have,

\displaystyle|||{Z_{(j_{0}+1)j}}|||_{D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+4)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3}\leq\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2})}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3}.

Similarly, by (
4.8
), one can obtain

\|X_{Z_{(j_{0}+1)j}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3},\ \ 3\leq j\leq j_{0}.

For
j=j_{0}+1
, from (
4.16
) and (
4.17
), we have

\displaystyle|||{Z_{(j_{0}+1)(j_{0}+1)}}|||_{D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}=|||{\widehat{Z_{j_{0}}}}|||_{D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|||{R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}}|||_{D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-2}.

Similarly, one can obtain

\|X_{Z_{(j_{0}+1)(j_{0}+1)}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq\|X_{R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-2}.

The estimate for
Z_{j_{0}+1}
has been established until now.

To obtain the remaining estimate, we consider the term
\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\{\{\breve{N},F_{j_{0}}\},F_{j_{0}}\}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}dt
. From Taylor’s formula, we have

\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\{\{\breve{N},F_{j_{0}}\},F_{j_{0}}\}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}dt=\sum_{j\geq 2}\frac{1}{j!}\breve{N}^{(j)},

where
\breve{N}^{(0)}=\breve{N}
,
\breve{N}^{(j)}=\{\breve{N}^{(j-1)},F_{j_{0}}\}
for
j\geq 1.
In addition,
\breve{N}^{(j)}
has the following form

\breve{N}^{(j)}=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+2-2j}\breve{N}^{(j)\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu}.

By homological equation (
4.14
), one gets

\breve{N}^{(1)}=-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+\widehat{Z_{j_{0}}}+\widehat{T_{j_{0}}}.

In what follows, we are going to give an estimate for

\widehat{T_{j_{0}}}=\{\breve{N},(1-\Gamma_{K})G_{j_{0}}\}.

Since

\{\breve{N},G_{j_{0}}\}=-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+\widehat{Z_{j_{0}}},

we have

\{[\breve{N}],G_{j_{0}}\}=-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+\widehat{Z_{j_{0}}}-\{\breve{N}-[\breve{N}],G_{j_{0}}\},

where
[\breve{N}]
denotes the mean value of the function
\breve{N}
with respect to the variable
x
over
\mathbb{T}^{n}
. Therefore,

\{[\breve{N}],(1-\Gamma_{K})G_{j_{0}}\}=(1-\Gamma_{K})(-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+\widehat{Z_{j_{0}}}-\{\breve{N}-[\breve{N}],G_{j_{0}}\}).

Let
K=\frac{\mathcal{M}}{\sigma^{\prime}}|\log\frac{1}{\rho}|=\mathcal{M}\mathcal{N}^{2}|\log\frac{1}{\rho}|
. It is easy to obtain

|||\{[\breve{N}],(1-\Gamma_{K})G_{j_{0}}\}|||_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{\mathcal{M}},

(4.31)

and

|||\{\breve{N}-[\breve{N}],(1-\Gamma_{K})G_{j_{0}}\}|||_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{\mathcal{M}}.

(4.32)

Combining (
4.31
) and (
4.32
), one obtains

|||\widehat{T_{j_{0}}}|||_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}=|||\{\breve{N},(1-\Gamma_{K})G_{j_{0}}\}|||_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{\mathcal{M}}.

(4.33)

Accordingly, one can obtain

\|X_{\widehat{T_{j_{0}}}}\|_{p-1,D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{\mathcal{M}}.

(4.34)

From (
4.4
), (
4.16
), (
4.17
) and (
4.33
), we get

|||{\breve{N}^{(1)}}|||_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq|||{R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}}|||_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}+|||\widehat{Z_{j_{0}}}|||_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}+|||\widehat{T_{j_{0}}}|||_{D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}.

\lessdot\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2})}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-2}.

(4.35)

Similarly, one can get

\|X_{\breve{N}^{(1)}}\|_{p-1,D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2})}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-2}.

(4.36)

For
2\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2
, let

\varrho_{j}=\frac{1}{12j\mathcal{N}^{2}},\quad\rho_{j}=\frac{\rho}{12j\mathcal{M}}.

Thus, by (
4.35
), (
4.29
) and Lemma
5.4
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{j!}|||{\breve{N}^{(j)}}|||_{D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}

\displaystyle\lessdot

\displaystyle\frac{1}{j!}\left(\frac{K^{n+1}(2\mathcal{N}+n)^{j_{0}+1-2}}{\varrho_{j}\rho_{j}\rho}\right)^{j-1}\cdot|||\breve{N}^{(1)}|||_{D_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\cdot(|||{F_{j_{0}}}|||_{D^{\prime\prime}_{j_{0}}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}^{*})^{j-1}

\displaystyle\lessdot

\displaystyle\frac{j^{2(j-1)}}{j!}\left(\frac{K^{n+1}(2\mathcal{N}+n)^{j_{0}-1}\mathcal{N}^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\right)^{j-1}\cdot\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-2}

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\rho^{2}\Big(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\Big)^{j_{0}-1}\right)^{j-1}

\displaystyle\lessdot

\displaystyle\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-2}\cdot\left(K^{n+1}\mathcal{N}^{2}\Big(\frac{(2\mathcal{N}+n)\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\Big)^{j_{0}-1}\right)^{j-1}

\displaystyle\lessdot

\displaystyle\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+5)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j_{0}-2}\cdot\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{(j_{0}-1)(j-1)}

\displaystyle\lessdot

\displaystyle\rho^{3}\Big(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\Big)^{j(j_{0}+1)-1-2j}=\rho^{3}\Big(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\Big)^{[j(j_{0}+1)+2-2j]-3},

(4.37)

where we use
j\leq\mathcal{M}+2
. Similarly, by using Cauchy estimates and (
4.36
), (
4.3
), we have

\frac{1}{j!}\|X_{\breve{N}^{(j)}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\Big(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\Big)^{[j(j_{0}+1)+2-2j]-3}.

(4.38)

Denote

j_{*}(j_{0}+1)+2-2j_{*}=\mathcal{M}+3.

Then, we can rewrite

\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\{\{\breve{N},F_{j_{0}}\},F_{j_{0}}\}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}dt=\sum_{2\leq j\leq j_{*}-1}\frac{1}{j!}\breve{N}^{(j)}+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{j_{*}}\breve{N}^{(j_{*})}\circ\Psi_{j_{0}}dt

Thus, the term
\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\{\{\breve{N},F_{j_{0}}\},F_{j_{0}}\}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}dt
is divided into two parts, the first part
\sum_{2\leq j\leq j_{*}-1}\frac{1}{j!}\breve{N}^{(j)}
can be divided into two functions

R^{(1)}=\sum_{2\leq j\leq j_{*}-1}\frac{1}{j!}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+2-2j\\
|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2\end{subarray}}\breve{N}^{(j)\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

and

Q^{(1)}=\sum_{2\leq j\leq j_{*}-1}\frac{1}{j!}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+2-2j\\
|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3\end{subarray}}\breve{N}^{(j)\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

where
R^{(1)}
contains the terms has variables
(z_{j},\bar{z}_{j})_{|j|\geq\mathcal{N}+1}
at most order
2
and
Q^{(1)}
contains the terms has variables
(z_{j},\bar{z}_{j})_{|j|\geq\mathcal{N}+1}
at least order
3
. Therefore,
R^{(1)}
and
Q^{(1)}
can be put into
R_{j_{0}+1}
and
Q_{j_{0}+1}
, respectively. The second part

P_{1}:=\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{j_{*}}\breve{N}^{(j_{*})}\circ\Psi_{j_{0}}dt

(4.39)

can be put into
T_{j_{0}+1}
. Moreover, from (
4.38
), we have the following estimate for
P_{1}

\|X_{P_{1}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}=\|X_{\breve{N}^{(j_{*})}\circ\Psi_{j_{0}}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\Big(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\Big)^{\mathcal{M}}.

(4.40)

Next, we will give the estimate for the term
\int_{0}^{1}\{R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Z_{j_{0}},F_{j_{0}}\}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}dt
. Let

W_{i}=Z_{j_{0}i},\quad 3\leq i\leq j_{0}

and

W_{j_{0}+1}=R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}.

Then

\sum_{3\leq i\leq j_{0}+1}W_{i}=R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Z_{j_{0}}.

Denote

W_{i}^{(0)}=W_{i}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}|_{t=0}=W_{i},

and

W_{i}^{(j)}=\{W_{i}^{(j-1)},F_{j_{0}}\},\quad j\geq 1.

By Taylor’s formula, we have

\int_{0}^{1}\{R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Z_{j_{0}},F_{j_{0}}\}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}dt=\sum_{3\leq i\leq j_{0}+1}(\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{1}{j!}W_{i}^{(j)}).

Note that
W_{i}^{(j)}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)
has the following form

W_{i}^{(j)}=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j}W_{i}^{(j)\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu}.

Using the discussion similar to
{\breve{N}^{(j)}}
(see (
4.37
), (
4.38
)) and the inequalities (
4.8
), (
4.4
), one obtains that, for
1\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2
,

\frac{1}{j!}|||{W_{i}^{(j)}}|||_{D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{(j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j)-3},

and

\frac{1}{j!}\|X_{W_{i}^{(j)}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{(j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j)-3}.

(4.41)

In addition, we have

2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j\geq j_{0}+2,

since
j\geq 1,j_{0}\geq 2,i\geq 3
.

For each
3\leq i\leq j_{0}+1
, we denote

j^{i}_{*}(j_{0}+1)+i-2j_{*}^{i}=\mathcal{M}+3.

Then, we rewrite the term

\int_{0}^{1}\{R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Z_{j_{0}},F_{j_{0}}\}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}dt

=\sum_{3\leq i\leq j_{0}+1}\left(\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_{*}^{i}}\frac{1}{j!}W_{i}^{(j)}+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{j^{i}_{*}}W_{i}^{(j_{*}^{i})}\circ\Psi_{j_{0}}dt\right)

As splitting the term
\sum_{2\leq j\leq j_{*}-1}\frac{1}{j!}\breve{N}^{(j)}
, we can write
\sum_{3\leq i\leq j_{0}+1}\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_{*}^{i}}\frac{1}{j!}W_{i}^{(j)}
into two functions
R^{(2)}
and
Q^{(2)}
with

R^{(2)}=\sum_{3\leq i\leq j_{0}+1}\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_{*}^{i}}\frac{1}{j!}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j\\
|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2\end{subarray}}W_{i}^{(j)\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

Q^{(2)}=\sum_{3\leq i\leq j_{0}+1}\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_{*}^{i}}\frac{1}{j!}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j\\
|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3\end{subarray}}W_{i}^{(j)\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

where
R^{(2)}
contains the terms has variables
(z_{j},\bar{z}_{j})_{|j|\geq\mathcal{N}+1}
at most order
2
and
Q^{(2)}
contains the terms has variables
(z_{j},\bar{z}_{j})_{|j|\geq\mathcal{N}+1}
at least order
3
. Therefore, we can put
R^{(2)}
and
Q^{(2)}
into
R_{j_{0}+1}
and
Q_{j_{0}+1}
, respectively. We denote the second term by

P_{2}:=\sum_{3\leq i\leq j_{0}+1}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{j^{i}_{*}}W_{i}^{(j_{*}^{i})}\circ\Psi_{j_{0}}dt.

(4.42)

Moreover, by (
4.41
), we have the estimate

\|X_{P_{2}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\Big(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\Big)^{\mathcal{M}}.

(4.43)

Finally, we give the estimate for the term

(R_{j_{0}}-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Q_{j_{0}})\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}|_{t=1}
. Let

U_{i}=R_{j_{0}i},\quad i\geq j_{0}+2,

and

V_{i}=Q_{j_{0}i},\quad i\geq 3.

Then

\sum_{i\geq j_{0}+2}U_{i}+\sum_{i\geq 3}V_{i}=R_{j_{0}}-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Q_{j_{0}}.

For simplicity, denote
A_{i}=U_{i}
or
V_{i}
. Let

A_{i}^{(0)}=A_{i}\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}|_{t=0}=A_{i},

and

A_{i}^{(j)}=\{A_{i}^{(j-1)},F_{j_{0}}\},\quad j\geq 1.

Using Taylor’s formula, we have

\displaystyle(R_{j_{0}}-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Q_{j_{0}})\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}|_{t=1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R_{j_{0}}-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Q_{j_{0}}+\sum_{i\geq j_{0}+2}(\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{1}{j!}U_{i}^{(j)})+\sum_{i\geq 3}(\sum_{j\geq 1}V_{i}^{(j)}).

For
j\geq 1
,
A_{i}^{(j)}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)
with
A_{i}=U_{i}
or
V_{i}
has the following form

A_{i}^{(j)}=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j}A_{i}^{(j)\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu}.

Using the discussion similar to
{\breve{N}^{(j)}}
(see (
4.37
), (
4.38
)) and the inequalities (
4.4
), (
4.10
), one obtains, for
j\leq\mathcal{M}+2
,

\frac{1}{j!}|||{A_{i}^{(j)}}|||_{D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{(j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j)-3},

and

\frac{1}{j!}\|X_{A_{i}^{(j)}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{(j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j)-3}.

(4.44)

In addition, we have

2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j\geq j_{0}+2,

since
j\geq 1,j_{0}\geq 2,i\geq 3
.

Let

j_{*}^{i}(j_{0}+1)+i-2j_{*}^{i}=\mathcal{M}+3.

Then we rewrite

\begin{split}&(R_{j_{0}}-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Q_{j_{0}})\circ X_{F_{j_{0}}}^{t}|_{t=1}\\
=&R_{j_{0}}-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+Q_{j_{0}}\\
&+\sum_{i\geq j_{0}+2}\left(\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_{*}^{i}}\frac{1}{j!}U_{i}^{(j)}+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{j^{i}_{*}}U_{i}^{(j_{*}^{i})}\circ\Psi_{j_{0}}dt\right)\\
&+\sum_{i\geq 3}\left(\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_{*}^{i}}\frac{1}{j!}V_{i}^{(j)}+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{j^{i}_{*}}V_{i}^{(j_{*}^{i})}\circ\Psi_{j_{0}}dt\right).\end{split}

We rewrite

\sum_{i\geq j_{0}+2}\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_{*}^{i}}\frac{1}{j!}U_{i}^{(j)}=R^{(3)}+Q^{(3)},

\sum_{i\geq 3}\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_{*}^{i}}\frac{1}{j!}V_{i}^{(j)}=R^{(4)}+Q^{(4)}

with

R^{(3)}=\sum_{i\geq j_{0}+2}\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_{*}^{i}}\frac{1}{j!}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j\\
|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2\end{subarray}}U_{i}^{(j)\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

Q^{(3)}=\sum_{i\geq j_{0}+2}\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_{*}^{i}}\frac{1}{j!}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j\\
|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3\end{subarray}}U_{i}^{(j)\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

R^{(4)}=\sum_{i\geq 3}\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_{*}^{i}}\frac{1}{j!}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j\\
|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2\end{subarray}}V_{i}^{(j)\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

Q^{(4)}=\sum_{i\geq 3}\sum_{1\leq j\leq j_{*}^{i}}\frac{1}{j!}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j(j_{0}+1)+i-2j\\
|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3\end{subarray}}V_{i}^{(j)\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu}.

Therefore, we can put the terms
R_{j_{0}}-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+R^{(3)}+R^{(4)}
and
Q_{j_{0}}+Q^{(3)}+Q^{(4)}
into
R_{j_{0}+1}
and
Q_{j_{0}+1}
, respectively. Denote

P_{3}=\sum_{i\geq j_{0}+2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{j^{i}_{*}}U_{i}^{(j_{*}^{i})}\circ\Psi_{j_{0}}dt+\sum_{i\geq 3}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)^{j^{i}_{*}}V_{i}^{(j_{*}^{i})}\circ\Psi_{j_{0}}dt.

(4.45)

By (
4.44
), we have the estimate

\|X_{P_{3}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\Big(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\Big)^{\mathcal{M}}.

(4.46)

As a consequence, we obtain that

R_{j_{0}+1}=R_{j_{0}}-R_{j_{0}(j_{0}+1)}+R^{(1)}+R^{(2)}+R^{(3)}+R^{(4)},

Q_{j_{0}+1}=Q_{j_{0}}+Q^{(1)}+Q^{(2)}+Q^{(3)}+Q^{(4)}.

In addition, from (
4.38
), (
4.41
) and (
4.44
), we have for
j\leq\mathcal{M}+2
,

\displaystyle\|X_{R_{(j_{0}+1)j}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}},,\ \frac{1}{\rho^{2}}|||{R_{(j_{0}+1)j}}|||_{D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3},

\displaystyle\|X_{Q_{(j_{0}+1)j}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}},\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}||||{Q_{(j_{0}+1)j}}|||_{D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3}.

We rewrite
T_{j_{0}+1}
as

T_{j_{0}+1}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\widehat{T_{j_{0}}}+T_{j_{0}}\circ\Psi_{j_{0}}+P_{1}+P_{2}+P_{3},

where
P_{1},P_{2},P_{3}
are defined by (
4.39
), (
4.42
) and (
4.45
).
Thus, noting (
4.34
), (
4.40
), (
4.43
) and (
4.46
), we finally obtain the estimates for
T_{j_{0}+1}

\|X_{T_{j_{0}+1}}\|_{p-1,D_{j_{0}+1}\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\Big(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(j_{0}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\Big)^{\mathcal{M}}.

Thus, we finish the proof of Lemma
4.3
.

4.5
Proof of Theorem
4.1

By Theorem
2.6
, we obtain a normal form of order
2
around KAM tori, which reads

\breve{H}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\breve{N}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+\breve{P}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

where

\breve{N}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\breve{\omega}_{j}(\xi)y_{j}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{\breve{\Omega}_{j}(x;\xi)}{j}z_{j}\bar{z}_{j},

and

\breve{P}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}^{n},\beta,\gamma\in\mathbb{N}^{\mathbb{N}},\\
2|\alpha|+|\beta+\gamma|\geq 3\end{subarray}}\breve{P}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}z^{\beta}\bar{z}^{\gamma}.

In the following, we use the notations in Lemma
4.3
. Let

H_{2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\breve{H}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

and write
\breve{P}

\breve{P}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=Z_{2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+R_{2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+Q_{2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+T_{2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

(4.47)

where

\displaystyle Z_{2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{3\leq j\leq 2}Z_{2j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=0,

\displaystyle R_{2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2}R_{2j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

\displaystyle Q_{2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2}Q_{2j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

\displaystyle T_{2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{j>\mathcal{M}+2}T_{2j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

(4.48)

with

Z_{2j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{2|\alpha|+2|\beta|+2|\mu|=j,|\mu|\leq 1}Z_{2}^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\beta}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\mu},

R_{2j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j,|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2}R_{2}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

Q_{2j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j,|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3}Q_{2}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

and

T_{2j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j}T_{2}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu}.

Denote
W_{j}(x,y,z,\bar{z}:\xi)=R_{2j}(x,y,z,\bar{z}:\xi)
,
Q_{2j}(x,y,z,\bar{z}:\xi)
or
T_{2j}(x,y,z,\bar{z}:\xi)
for
j\geq 3
.
Then, by the definition of the norm
|||\cdot|||
in definition
2.3
and (
3.34
), one gets

|||{W_{j}}|||_{D(s_{0}/2,5\rho,5\rho)\times\Pi_{\eta}}\leq|||{\breve{P}}|||_{D(s_{0}/2,r_{0}/2,r_{0}/2)\times\Pi_{\eta}}\left(\frac{10\rho}{r_{0}}\right)^{j}\leq\epsilon(1+c\eta^{6}\varepsilon)\left(\frac{10\rho}{r_{0}}\right)^{j}.

By the choice of
\mathcal{N}
(noting that larg
\mathcal{N}
is depending on
s_{0},r_{0},n,\mathcal{M},\tau
and
p
) and
0<\breve{\eta}<1
, we have

|||{W_{j}}|||_{D(s_{0}/2,5\rho,5\rho)\times\Pi_{\eta}}\leq\rho^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3},\quad j\geq 3.

From Lemma
5.6
, it implies that

\displaystyle\|X_{W_{j}}\|_{p-1,D(5\varrho,5\rho,5\rho)\times\Pi_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle c^{j-2}\frac{j^{p+2}}{(\frac{s_{0}}{2}-5\varrho)^{p}}|||W_{j}|||_{D(s_{0}/2,5\rho,5\rho)\times\Pi_{\eta}}\rho^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3},\quad j\geq 3.

(4.49)

By (
4.47
), (
4.48
) and (
4.49
), we have

\displaystyle\|X_{T_{2}}\|_{p-1,D(5\varrho,5\rho,5\rho)\times\Pi_{\eta}}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\geq\mathcal{M}+3}\|X_{T_{2j}}\|_{p-1,D(5\varrho,5\rho,5\rho)\times\Pi_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c\sum_{j\geq\mathcal{M}+3}\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3}\leq\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{\mathcal{M}}.

Let
\Psi=\Psi_{2}\circ\cdots\circ\Psi_{\mathcal{M}+1}
.
Then by Lemma
4.3
, one gets

\breve{\breve{H}}=\breve{H}\circ\Phi=\breve{N}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+Z_{\mathcal{M}+2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)

+R_{\mathcal{M}+2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+Q_{\mathcal{M}+2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+T_{\mathcal{M}+2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

where

\displaystyle Z_{\mathcal{M}+2}(y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2}Z_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

\displaystyle Q_{\mathcal{M}+2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2}Q_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

\displaystyle T_{\mathcal{M}+2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{j\geq 3}T_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

\displaystyle R_{\mathcal{M}+2}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=0,

with

\displaystyle Z_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}(y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|+2|\beta|+2|\mu|=j,|\mu|=0}Z_{\mathcal{M}+2}^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\beta}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\mu}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|+2|\beta|+2|\mu|=j,|\mu|=1}Z_{\mathcal{M}+2}^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\beta}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\mu},

Q_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j,|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3}Q_{\mathcal{M}+2}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu}.

T_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j}T_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},\quad j\geq\mathcal{M}+3,

and

T_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|=j\\
\beta\neq\gamma,\mu\neq\nu,|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2\end{subarray}}T_{j_{0}}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},\quad 3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2.

Moreover, the functions
Z_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)
,
Q_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)
and
T_{(\mathcal{M}+2)}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)
satisfy the following estimates,

\displaystyle\|X_{{Z_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}}}\|_{p-1,D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}},\ \frac{1}{\rho^{2}}|||{Z_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}}|||_{D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3},

\displaystyle\|X_{Q_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}}\|_{p-1,D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}},\ \frac{1}{\rho^{2}}|||{Q_{(\mathcal{M}+2)j}}|||_{D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{j-3},

\displaystyle\|X_{T_{(\mathcal{M}+2)}}\|_{p-1,D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\lessdot\rho\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho\right)^{\mathcal{M}}.

By the choice of
\mathcal{N}
, we have

\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\rho<\frac{1}{2}.

Then by the definition of
\Psi
, we have

\|\Psi-id\|_{p,D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)}\leq c\frac{\rho\mathcal{N}^{C}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}},

||D\Psi-Id||_{p,D(4\varrho,4\rho,4\rho)}\leq c\frac{\mathcal{N}^{C}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}},

where
C
is some constant depending on
n
and
\tau
.

4.6
Measure estimate

In this section, we will show

{\rm Meas}\ \Pi_{\acute{\eta}}\geq({\rm Meas}\ \Pi_{\eta})(1-c\acute{\eta}),

where
c
is a constant depending on
n
.

Firstly, we want to compute the number of the non-empty non-resonant sets
\mathcal{R}_{k\acute{l}\hat{l}}
defined by (
4.1
). For this purpose, we consider the term
\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle
which is classified into the following three cases:

Case 1
:
|\hat{l}|=1
and
|\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|=|[\breve{\Omega}_{i}]|
, for some
i\geq\mathcal{N}+1.

Then, it follows that

|\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|\geq\frac{c_{1}}{2}i^{2}\geq\frac{c_{1}}{2}i.

Case 2
:
|\hat{l}|=2
and
|\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|=|[\breve{\Omega}_{i}]+[\breve{\Omega}_{j}]|
or
|\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|=|[\breve{\Omega}_{i}]-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}]|
, (
i\neq\pm j
).
Then

|\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|\geq c_{1}(i^{2}\pm j^{2})-(i+j)c\eta^{6}\epsilon\geq\frac{c_{1}}{2}|i+j||i-j|\geq\frac{c_{1}}{2}\max\{|i|,|j|\}.

In case 1 and case 2, assume
|\Omega_{j}(\xi)|\leq c_{2}|j|^{2}
. Thus, if

\max\{|i|,|j|\}\geq\frac{8}{c_{1}}\left(|k||\breve{\omega}(\xi)|+c_{2}(\mathcal{M}+2)\mathcal{N}^{2}\right),

then

\displaystyle|\langle k,\breve{\omega}\rangle+\langle\acute{l},[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle+\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|\geq|\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|-|\langle k,\breve{\omega}\rangle+\langle\acute{l},[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{1}}{4}\max\{|i|,|j|\}+2(|k||\breve{\omega}(\xi)|+c_{2}(\mathcal{M}+2)\mathcal{N}^{2}+1)

\displaystyle-(1+c\eta^{6}\epsilon)(|k||\breve{\omega}(\xi)|+c_{2}|\acute{l}|\mathcal{N}^{2})\geq\frac{c_{1}}{4}\max\{|i|,|j|\},

which implies
\mathcal{R}_{k\acute{l}\hat{l}}=\emptyset
for
\max\{|i|,|j|\}\geq\frac{8}{c_{1}}\left(|k||\breve{\omega}(\xi)|+c_{2}(\mathcal{M}+2)\mathcal{N}^{2}\right)
. Therefore, for the above two cases, we only need to consider

|i|,|j|\leq j_{*}:=\frac{8}{c_{1}}\left(|k||\omega(\xi)|+c_{2}(\mathcal{M}+2)\mathcal{N}^{2}\right).

It means that, for the above two cases, the number of non empty sets
\mathcal{R}_{k\acute{l}\hat{l}}
is less than

A_{1}:=(2\mathcal{N}+1)^{|\acute{l}|}\left(\frac{8}{c_{1}}(|k||\omega(\xi)|+c_{2}(\mathcal{M}+2)\mathcal{N}^{2})\right)^{2}.

Case 3
:
|\hat{l}|=2
,
|\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle|=|[\breve{\Omega}_{i}]-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}]|
for some
|i|,|j|\geq\mathcal{N}+1
and
i=-j.
In this case, by momentum conservation, for the set
\mathcal{R}_{k\acute{l}\hat{l}}
we have the relation

-\sum_{b=1}^{n}j_{b}k_{b}-2j+\sum_{|i|\leq\mathcal{N}}i(\beta_{i}-\gamma_{i})=0,

(4.50)

where
\acute{l}=(\beta,\gamma)
. It implies that
j
is bounded by

|j|\leq\frac{1}{2}|k|\max_{b}\{j_{b}\}+\frac{1}{2}(\mathcal{M}+2)\mathcal{N}\leq j_{**}:=C|k|+\frac{1}{2}(\mathcal{M}+2)\mathcal{N}.

It means that for cases 3 the number of non empty sets
\mathcal{R}_{k\acute{l}\hat{l}}
is less than

A_{2}:=(2\mathcal{N}+1)^{|\acute{l}|}(C|k|+\frac{1}{2}(\mathcal{M}+2)\mathcal{N})^{2}.

Remark 4.6
.

From equation (
4.50
), it is easy to observe that if
|k|=|\acute{l}|=0
, we must have
j=0
. That is, there is no such set
\mathcal{R}_{k\acute{l}\hat{l}}
with
|k|=|\acute{l}|=0
and
i=-j
.

In the following, we are going to estimate the measure of
\mathcal{R}_{k\acute{l}\hat{l}}
.

For
k\neq 0
,
|i|,|j|<\max\{j_{*},j_{**}\}
, denote

j_{max}=\max\{j:\acute{l}_{j}\,{\rm or}\,\hat{l}_{j}\neq 0\},\quad j_{min}=\min\{j:\acute{l}_{j}\,{\rm or}\,\hat{l}_{j}\neq 0\}

J=\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}j_{max},\quad{\rm if}\ j_{max}>-j_{min},\\
j_{min},\quad{\rm if}\ j_{max}\leq-j_{min}.\\
\end{array}\right.

(4.51)

Without loss of generality, we assume
\acute{l}_{J}\neq 0
.

For
|k|<|J|
, in view of (
3.31
), (
3.33
) and
2\mathcal{M}\epsilon\eta^{6}<\frac{1}{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\partial_{\xi_{J}}(\langle k,\breve{\omega}\rangle+\langle\acute{l},[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle+\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle)|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle|\acute{l}_{J}||\partial_{\xi_{J}}[\acute{\Omega}]_{J}|-|\partial_{\xi_{J}}(\langle k,\breve{\omega}\rangle+\langle\acute{l},[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle+\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle-\acute{l}_{J}[\acute{\Omega}]_{J})|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle|\acute{l}_{J}|(1-c\eta^{6}\epsilon)-|k|\frac{c\eta\epsilon}{|J|}-\sum_{j\neq J}|\acute{l}_{j}|\frac{|j|c\eta^{6}\epsilon}{|J|}-\sum_{j\geq\mathcal{N},|\hat{l}|\leq 2}|\hat{l}_{j}|\frac{|j|c\eta^{6}\epsilon}{|J|}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle|\acute{l}_{J}|-|k|\frac{c\eta^{6}\epsilon}{|J|}-(\mathcal{M}+2)c\eta^{6}\epsilon

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle|\acute{l}_{J}|-[c\eta^{6}\epsilon+(\mathcal{M}+2)c\eta^{6}\epsilon]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}.

Hence

{\rm Meas}\,\mathcal{R}_{k\acute{l}\hat{l}}\leq\frac{4\acute{\eta}M_{\acute{l},\hat{l}}}{4^{\mathcal{M}}(|k|+1)^{\tau}C(\mathcal{N},\acute{l})}{\rm Meas}\,\Pi_{\eta}.

For
|k|\geq|J|
, let

|k_{1}|=\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\{|k_{1}|,\cdots,|k_{n}|\}.

Then, by
2n\mathcal{M}\epsilon\eta^{6}<\frac{1}{2}
, we have

\displaystyle|\partial_{\xi_{1}}(\langle k,\breve{\omega}\rangle+\langle\acute{l},[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle+\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle)|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle|k_{1}||\partial_{\xi_{1}}\breve{\omega}_{1}|-|\partial_{\xi_{1}}(\sum_{i=2}^{n}k_{i},\breve{\omega}_{i}+\langle\acute{l},[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle+\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle)|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle|k_{1}|(1-c\eta^{6}\epsilon)-\sum_{i=2}^{n}|k_{i}|c\eta^{6}\epsilon-(|\acute{l}|+|\hat{l}|)c\eta^{6}\epsilon\cdot|J|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle|k_{1}|-|k|c\eta^{6}\epsilon-(\mathcal{M}+2)c\eta^{6}\epsilon|J|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle|k_{1}|-n|k_{1}|c\eta^{6}\epsilon-(\mathcal{M}+2)c\eta^{6}\epsilon|k|\quad(|J|\leq|k|,\quad|k|\leq n|k_{1}|)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}|k_{1}|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}.

Hence

{\rm Meas}\,\mathcal{R}_{k\acute{l}\hat{l}}\leq\frac{4\ \acute{\eta}M_{\acute{l},\hat{l}}}{4^{\mathcal{M}}(|k|+1)^{\tau}C(\mathcal{N},\acute{l})}{\rm Meas}\,\Pi_{\eta}.

If
|k|=0
and
|i|,|j|<\max\{j_{*},j_{**}\}
, we let
J\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\{0\}
be a number defined in (
4.51
). Without loss of generality, we assume
\acute{l}_{J}\neq 0
. Then

\displaystyle|\partial_{\xi_{J}}(\langle k,\breve{\omega}\rangle+\langle\acute{l},[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle+\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle)|\geq

\displaystyle|\acute{l}_{J}||\partial_{\xi_{J}}[\acute{\Omega}]_{J}|-|\partial_{\xi_{J}}(\langle\acute{l},[\acute{\Omega}]\rangle+\langle\hat{l},[\hat{\Omega}]\rangle-\acute{l}_{J}[\acute{\Omega}]_{J})|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle|\breve{l}_{J}|(1-c\eta\epsilon)-(\sum_{j\neq J}|\acute{l}_{j}|\frac{|j|c\eta^{6}\epsilon}{|J|}+\sum_{j\geq\mathcal{N},|\hat{l}|\leq 2}|\hat{l}|_{j}\frac{|j|c\eta^{6}\epsilon}{|J|})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle|\breve{l}_{J}|-(\mathcal{M}+2)c\eta^{6}\epsilon

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4},\quad(\mathcal{M}\leq(2c\eta^{6}\epsilon)^{-1}).

Hence,

{\rm Meas}\,\mathcal{R}_{k\acute{l}\hat{l}}\leq\frac{4\acute{\eta}M_{\acute{l},\hat{l}}}{4^{\mathcal{M}}(|k|+1)^{\tau}C(\mathcal{N},\acute{l})}{\rm Meas}\,\Pi_{\eta}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle{\rm Meas}\,\mathcal{R}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|k|+|\acute{l}|+|\hat{l}|\neq 0,\\
|\acute{l}|+|\hat{l}|\leq\mathcal{M}+2,|\hat{l}|\leq 2\end{subarray}}{\rm Meas}\,\mathcal{R}_{k\acute{l}\hat{l}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}^{n},|\acute{l}|\leq\mathcal{M}+2}\frac{4\acute{\eta}M_{\acute{l},\hat{l}}(A_{1}+A_{2})}{4^{\mathcal{M}}(|k|+1)^{\tau}C(\mathcal{N},\acute{l})}\ {\rm Meas}\,\Pi_{\eta}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}^{n},|\acute{l}|\leq\mathcal{M}+2}\frac{4\acute{\eta}\max\{j_{*},j_{**},\mathcal{N}\}(A_{1}+A_{2})}{4^{\mathcal{M}}(|k|+1)^{\tau}C(\mathcal{N},\acute{l})}\ {\rm Meas}\,\Pi_{\eta}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c\acute{\eta}\,{\rm Meas}\,\Pi_{\eta},

where
c
is a constant depending on
c_{1},c_{2}
and
n
. Thus, we finish the proof of measure estimate.

4.7
Proof of Theorem
2.8

Based on Theorem
4.1
, for a given positive integer
0\leq\mathcal{M}\leq(c\eta^{6}\epsilon)^{-1/2}
and
0<\acute{\eta}<1
, there exists a small
\delta_{0}
depending on
s_{0},r_{0},n,\acute{\eta}
and
\mathcal{M}
such that, for each
0<\delta<\delta_{0}
,
\xi\in\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}
and the positive
\mathcal{N}
satisfying

\delta^{-\frac{\mathcal{M}+1}{2(p-1)}}\leq\mathcal{N}+1<\delta^{-\frac{\mathcal{M}+1}{2(p-1)}}+1,

(4.52)

and there is a map

\Phi:D(4\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\rightarrow D(5\varrho,5\delta,5\delta),

such that

\begin{split}\breve{\breve{H}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)&=\breve{H}\circ\Phi=\breve{N}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+Z(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)\\
&+Q(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+T(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),\end{split}

with

\breve{N}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\breve{\omega}_{j}(\xi)y_{j}+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}_{*}}\frac{\breve{\Omega}_{j}(x;\xi)}{j}z_{j}\bar{z}_{j},

(4.53)

\displaystyle Z(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=

\displaystyle\sum_{4\leq 2|\alpha|+2|\beta|+2|\mu|\leq\mathcal{M}+2,|\mu|=0}Z^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\beta}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\mu}

\displaystyle+\sum_{4\leq 2|\alpha|+2|\beta|+2|\mu|\leq\mathcal{M}+2,|\mu|=1}Z^{\alpha\beta\beta\mu\mu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\beta}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\mu},

(4.54)

Q(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=\sum_{3\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|\leq\mathcal{M}+2,|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3}Q^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu}.

and

\displaystyle T(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=

\displaystyle\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|\geq\mathcal{M}+3}T^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}3\leq 2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|\leq\mathcal{M}+2\\
\beta\neq\gamma,\mu\neq\nu,|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2\end{subarray}}T^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu}.

Moreover, the following estimates hold:

(1) for each
\xi\in\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}
, the symplectic map
\Phi
satisfies

\|\Phi-id\|_{p,D(4\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq\frac{c\mathcal{N}^{C}\delta}{\acute{\eta}^{2}},

||D\Phi-Id||_{p,D(4\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq\frac{c\mathcal{N}^{C}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}.

(2)The functions
{Z},Q
and
T
satisfy

\|X_{Z_{j}}\|_{p-1,D(4\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}},\,\frac{1}{\delta^{2}}|||Z_{j}|||_{D(4\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq c\delta\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\delta\right)^{j-3},

(4.55)

\|X_{Q_{j}}\|_{p-1,D(4\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}},\,\frac{1}{\delta^{2}}|||Q_{j}|||_{D(4\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq c\delta\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\delta\right)^{j-3},

(4.56)

and

\|X_{T}\|_{p-1,D(4\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq c\delta\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\delta\right)^{\mathcal{M}},

(4.57)

where
c
is a constant depending on
s_{0},r_{0},n,\mathcal{M}
.

By (
4.55
),(
4.56
) and (
4.57
), we have

\displaystyle\|X_{T}\|_{p-1,D(4\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq

\displaystyle c\delta\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\delta\right)^{\mathcal{M}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1}\left(\frac{c\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}\mathcal{M}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2\mathcal{M}}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{mathn}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1}\left(\frac{c}{\acute{\eta}^{2\mathcal{M}}}\delta^{-\frac{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}\mathcal{M}(\mathcal{M}+1)}{p-1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1}\left(\frac{c}{\acute{\eta}^{2\mathcal{M}}}\delta^{\frac{1}{4}}\right)

\displaystyle({\rm by}\ \ p\geq 8C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{4}+1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1},

(4.58)

\displaystyle\|X_{Q}\|_{p-1,D(4\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\geq 3}c\delta\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\delta\right)^{j-3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\geq 3}c\delta\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\delta\right)^{j-3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta,

and

\displaystyle\|X_{Z}\|_{p-1,D(4\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\geq 4}c\delta\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\delta\right)^{j-3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\geq 4}c\delta\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+6)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\delta\right)^{j-3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{2}.

In addition, one can obtain the following inequality from (
4.52
)

\displaystyle\|\hat{z}\|_{1}=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{j\geq\mathcal{N}+1}|z_{j}|^{2}j^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{j\geq\mathcal{N}+1}|j|^{2p}|z_{j}|^{2p}/j^{2(p-1)}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\|\hat{z}\|_{p}}{(\mathcal{N}+1)^{p-1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1}\|\hat{z}\|_{p}.

(4.59)

By Lemma
5.6
, we get on the domain
D(3\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\delta}\|(-{\bf i}m\partial_{z_{m}}Q)_{m\in\mathbb{Z}_{*}}\|_{p/2}\leq

\displaystyle c\sum_{3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2}|||Q_{j}|||_{D(s,r,r)\times\Pi}(4\delta)^{-(j+1)},

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{\alpha}(\frac{1}{(\varrho)^{p/2}}|y|^{|\alpha|}\|z\|_{1}^{j-2|\alpha|-1}+\|z\|_{p}\|z\|_{1}^{j-2|\alpha|-2}),

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c\sum_{3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2}\delta^{3}\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\delta\right)^{j-3}(4\delta)^{-(j+1)}

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{\alpha}\left(\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2}}{\mathcal{M}}\right)^{p/2}(4\delta)^{j-1}\delta^{2(\mathcal{M}+1)}+(4\delta)^{j-1}\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1}\right)

\displaystyle({\rm since}\ Q_{j}=O(|\hat{z}|^{3})\ {\rm and}\ \eqref{2p2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1}\sum_{3\leq j\leq\mathcal{M}+2}c\delta\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}^{2C_{0}(\mathcal{M}+7)^{2}}}{\acute{\eta}^{2}}\delta\right)^{j-3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1},

where
c
is a constant depending on
s_{0},n,r_{0}
and
\mathcal{M}
.
Similarly, the following estimates hold

\frac{1}{(4\delta)^{2}}\|\partial_{x}Q\|,\quad\|\partial_{y}Q\|,\quad\frac{1}{4\delta}\|({\bf i}m\partial_{\bar{z}_{m}}Q)_{m\in\mathbb{Z}_{*}}\|_{p/2}\leq\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1}.

In conclusion, we have

\displaystyle\|X_{Q}\|_{p-1,D(3\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1}.

(4.60)

Observe that the function
Z
in the Hamiltonian
\breve{\breve{H}}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)
depends on the angular variable
x
. Therefore, the above normal form is not enough to give the long time stability of KAM tori. In order to prove the long time stability of KAM tori, we will do some further transformations. By the definition of
\breve{N}
and
Z
in (
4.53
) and (
4.54
), we can rewrite the Hamiltonian
\breve{\breve{H}}
as

\breve{\breve{H}}=\breve{N}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+Z(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+Q(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)+T(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi),

with

\displaystyle\breve{N}(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\breve{\omega}_{j}(\xi)y_{j}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{[\breve{\Omega}_{j}](\xi)}{j}|z_{j}|^{2}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{(\breve{\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}])(x;\xi)}{j}(|z_{j}|^{2}-|z_{j}(0)|^{2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{(\breve{\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}])(x;\xi)}{j}|z_{j}(0)|^{2},

and

\displaystyle Z(x,y,z,\bar{z};\xi)=

\displaystyle\sum_{4\leq 2|\alpha|+2|\beta|\leq\mathcal{M}+2,|\mu|\leq 1}Z^{\alpha\beta}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}|z|^{2\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{4\leq 2|\alpha|+2|\beta|\leq\mathcal{M}+2,|\mu|\leq 1}Z^{\alpha\beta}(x)y^{\alpha}(|z|^{2}-|z(0)|^{2}+|z(0)|^{2})^{\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 2}Z^{\alpha}(\xi)y^{\alpha}+\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 1,|\beta|\geq 1}Z^{\alpha\beta}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}|z(0)|^{2\beta}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|,|\gamma|\geq 0,|\beta|\geq 1}\widetilde{Z}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}|z(0)|^{2\gamma}(|z|^{2}-|z(0)|^{2})^{\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 2}\widetilde{Z}^{\alpha}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}+\sum_{|\alpha|,|\gamma|\geq 0,|\beta|\geq 1}\widetilde{Z}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}|z(0)|^{2\gamma}(|z|^{2}-|z(0)|^{2})^{\beta},

where

\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\widetilde{Z}^{\alpha}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}=\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 2}Z^{\alpha}(\xi)y^{\alpha}+\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 1,|\beta|\geq 1}Z^{\alpha\beta}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}|z(0)|^{2\beta}.

Therefore, the Hamiltonian becomes

\displaystyle\breve{\breve{H}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\breve{\omega}_{j}(\xi)y_{j}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{[\breve{\Omega}_{j}](\xi)}{j}|z_{j}|^{2}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{(\breve{\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}])(x;\xi)}{j}(|z_{j}|^{2}-|z_{j}(0)|^{2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{(\breve{\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}])(x;\xi)}{j}|z_{j}(0)|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\widetilde{Z}^{\alpha}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|,|\gamma|\geq 0,|\beta|\geq 1}\widetilde{Z}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}|z|^{2\gamma}(|z|^{2}-|z(0)|^{2})^{\beta}+Q+T

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N+A+\widetilde{Z}+B+Q+T,

where

\displaystyle N=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\breve{\omega}_{j}(\xi)y_{j}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{[\breve{\Omega}_{j}](\xi)}{j}|z_{j}|^{2}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{(\breve{\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}])(x;\xi)}{j}(|z_{j}|^{2}-|z_{j}(0)|^{2})

\displaystyle A=

\displaystyle\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{(\breve{\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}])(x;\xi)}{j}|z_{j}(0)|^{2}+\widetilde{Z}^{1}(x;\xi)y

\displaystyle\widetilde{Z}=

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 2}\widetilde{Z}^{\alpha}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha},\quad B=\sum_{|\alpha|,|\gamma|\geq 0,|\beta|\geq 1}\widetilde{Z}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}|z(0)|^{2\gamma}(|z|^{2}-|z(0)|^{2})^{\beta},

\displaystyle Q=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}3\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|\leq\mathcal{M}+2\\
|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3\end{subarray}}Q^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

\displaystyle T=

\displaystyle\sum_{2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|\geq\mathcal{M}+3}T^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

\displaystyle+\sum_{3\leq\begin{subarray}{c}2|\alpha|+|\beta|+|\gamma|+|\mu|+|\nu|\leq\mathcal{M}+2\\
\beta\neq\gamma,\mu\neq\nu,|\mu|+|\nu|\leq 2\end{subarray}}T^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

where
Q
and
T
satisfying the inequalities (
4.60
) and (
4.57
), respectively.

Since

\sup_{j}\left\|\frac{(\breve{\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}])(x;\xi)}{j}\right\|_{D(4\varrho)\times\Pi}\leq c\eta^{6}\epsilon,

and
\|z(0)\|_{p}\leq\delta,

we have

\left\|\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{(\breve{\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}])(x;\xi)}{j}|z_{j}(0)|^{2}\right\|_{D(4\varrho)\times\Pi}\leq\delta^{2}.

From the estimate of
Z
in (
4.55
), one can easily obtain

\|\widetilde{Z}^{1}(x;\xi)\|_{D(4\varrho)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq\delta^{2-},

and

\|\widetilde{Z}^{\alpha}(x;\xi)-[\widetilde{Z}^{\alpha}](\xi)\|_{D(4\varrho)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq\delta^{2-},\quad{\rm for}\ \ |\alpha|\geq 2.

Thus, on the domain
D(3\varrho,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}

\|X_{A(x,y;\xi)}\|_{D(3\varrho,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq\delta^{2-}.

(4.61)

\|X_{\widetilde{Z}(x,y;\xi)}\|_{D(3\varrho,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq\delta^{2-},

(4.62)

where the domain
D(s,r):=\{(x,y):|{\rm Im}\ x|\leq s,|y|\leq r^{2}\}
. In addition, for the function
B
, if we let

B=\sum_{|\beta|\geq 1}B^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)(|z|^{2}-|z(0)|^{2})^{\beta},\quad B^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)=\sum_{|\alpha|,|\gamma|\geq 0}\widetilde{Z}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}|z(0)|^{\gamma}.

From (
4.55
) and the definition of
B
, we can easily to compute

\|B^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)\|_{D(3\varrho,4\delta)\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq\delta^{2-}.

(4.63)

We rewrite the function
A
by
A(x,y)=\Gamma_{K}A(x,y)+(1-\Gamma_{K})A(x,y)
with

K=\left|\frac{1}{\varrho}\log\delta^{-\mathcal{M}}\right|

(4.64)

and here
\Gamma_{K}A
denotes the Fourier series of
A=A(x,y)
truncated at
K
-th order. Then, on the domain
D(2\varrho,3\delta)
, we have
\|X_{(1-\Gamma_{K})A(x,y)}\|\leq\delta^{\mathcal{M}+2}
.

In the following, we will perform some transformations such that
A(x,y)
become small enough for long time stability estimate. For this purpose, we will perform KAM iteration in
(x,y)
-variables. Let
D_{j}=\{(x,y):|{\rm Im}\ x|\leq\varrho_{j},|y|\leq\delta_{j}^{2}\}
where
\varrho_{j}=3\varrho-j\varrho/\log_{2}\mathcal{M}
and
\delta_{j}=4\delta-j\delta/\log_{2}\mathcal{M}
.

Let
\Phi=X_{F}^{t}|_{t=1}
with

F=F^{0}(x)+F^{1}(x)y.

Then, we obtain

\displaystyle\breve{\breve{H}}\circ\Phi=

\displaystyle(N+A+\widetilde{Z}+B+T+Q)\circ\Phi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N+\{N,F\}+\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\{\{N,F\},F\}\circ X_{F}^{t}dt

\displaystyle+\Gamma_{K}A+(1-\Gamma_{K})A+\int_{0}^{t}t\{A,F\}\circ X_{F}^{t}dt

\displaystyle+(\widetilde{Z}+B+Q+T)\circ\Phi.

(4.65)

Then, we want to solve the following homological equation.

\{N,F\}=\Gamma_{K}A-[A],

where
[A]=[\widetilde{Z}^{1}]y
. Here, the notation
[\cdot]
represents the average of a function with respect to the variable
x
over
\mathbb{T}^{n}
. By Fourier expansion, in both sides of the homological equation, we have

\sum_{0\neq|k|\leq K}{\bf i}\langle k,\breve{\omega}\rangle\widehat{F^{0}_{k}}e^{{\bf i}\langle k,x\rangle}=\sum_{0\neq|k|\leq K}\widehat{A^{0}_{k}}e^{{\bf i}\langle k,x\rangle},

\sum_{0\neq|k|\leq K}{\bf i}\langle k,\breve{\omega}\rangle\widehat{F^{1}_{k}}e^{{\bf i}\langle k,x\rangle}=\sum_{0\neq|k|\leq K}\widehat{A^{1}_{k}}e^{{\bf i}\langle k,x\rangle}.

Thus, we have

{\bf i}\langle k,\breve{\omega}\rangle\widehat{F^{0}_{k}}=\widehat{A^{0}_{k}},\quad\ {\bf i}\langle k,\breve{\omega}\rangle\widehat{F^{1}_{k}}=\widehat{A^{1}_{k}}.

For each
\xi\in\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}
,
if
\breve{\omega}
satisfies the Diophantine condition, i.e.,

|\langle k,\breve{\omega}\rangle|\geq\frac{\acute{\eta}}{1+|k|^{\tau}},

(4.66)

then one can easily obtain

\displaystyle\|X_{F}\|_{D_{1}}\leq\acute{\eta}^{-1}K^{\tau}\delta^{2}.

(4.67)

It implies the transformation
\Phi:D_{1}\rightarrow D_{0}
.
Then, we are going to estimate the remaining terms in (
4.65
). By (
4.61
) and (
4.67
), we have

\Big\|X_{\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\{\{N,F\},F\}\circ X_{F}^{t}dt}\Big\|_{D_{1}}\leq\delta^{4-},

\Big\|X_{\int_{0}^{1}t\{A,F\}\circ X_{F}^{t}dt}\Big\|_{D_{1}}\leq\delta^{4-}.

Under the transformation
\Phi
and by (
4.62
), the function
\tilde{Z}=\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 2}\tilde{Z}^{\alpha}y^{\alpha}
has the estimate

\displaystyle\|X_{\tilde{Z}\circ\Phi-\tilde{Z}}\|_{D(4\varrho,4\delta)}=\Big\|\int_{0}^{1}t\{\tilde{Z},F\}\circ X_{F}^{t}dt\Big\|_{D(4\varrho)}\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{4-}.

For the term
B,Q
, we still have the form

B\circ\Phi=\sum_{|\beta|\geq 1}B_{+}^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)(|z|^{2}-|z(0)|^{2})^{\beta},

Q\circ\Phi=\sum_{|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3}Q_{+}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

since we only do change variables for the variables
x,y
. For the term
B,Q
and
T
, since transformation
\Phi:D(\varrho_{1},\delta_{1},4\delta)\rightarrow D(3\varrho,4\delta,4\delta)
and the estimate (
4.58
), (
4.60
) and (
4.63
), we have

\|B_{+}^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)\|_{D(\varrho_{1},\delta_{1})\times\Pi_{\acute{\eta}}}\leq\delta^{2-}.

\|X_{Q\circ\Phi}\|_{p/2,D(\varrho_{1},\delta_{1},4\delta)},\quad\|X_{T\circ\Phi}\|_{p-1,D(\varrho_{1},\delta_{1},4\delta)}\leq\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1}.

Thus, under the transformation
\Phi
, the Hamiltonian becomes

H=N_{+}+A_{+}+Z_{+}+B_{+}+Q_{+}+T_{+},

with

N_{+}=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\omega_{+j}(\xi)y_{j}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{[\breve{\Omega}_{j}](\xi)}{j}|z_{j}|^{2}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{\breve{(\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}])(x;\xi)}{j}(|z_{j}|^{2}-|z_{j}(0)|^{2}),

A_{+}=A_{+}^{0}(x)+A_{+}^{1}(x)y,\quad Z_{+}=\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 2}Z_{+}^{\alpha}(x)y^{\alpha},

B_{+}=\sum_{|\beta|\geq 1}B_{+}^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)(|z|^{2}-|z(0)|^{2})^{\beta},

Q_{+}=\sum_{|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3}Q_{+}^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

where the functions
A_{+},\ Z_{+},B_{+},Q_{+}
and
T_{+}
satisfies
\|X_{A_{+}}\|_{D_{1}}\leq\delta^{4-},\ \|X_{Z_{+}}\|_{D_{1}}\leq\delta^{2-}
,
\|B_{+}^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)\|_{D(\varrho_{1},\delta_{1})}\leq\delta^{2-}
and
\|X_{Q_{+}}\|_{p/2,D(\varrho_{1},\delta_{1},4\delta)},\|X_{T_{+}}\|_{p-1,D(\varrho_{1},\delta_{1},4\delta)}\leq\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1}
, respectively. Here,

\omega_{+}=\breve{\omega}+[\tilde{Z}^{1}].

In what follows, we will verify the new frequency
\omega_{+}
satisfy the condition

|\langle k,\omega_{+}\rangle|\geq\frac{\acute{\eta}}{2(1+|k|^{\tau})},\quad 0\neq k\in\mathbb{Z}^{n},

if
\breve{\omega}
satisfies the Diophantine condition (
4.66
). Actually, by (
4.66
), one derives

\displaystyle|\langle k,\omega_{+}\rangle|\geq

\displaystyle|\langle k,\breve{\omega}\rangle|-|\langle k,\omega_{+}-\breve{\omega}\rangle|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\acute{\eta}}{1+|k|^{\tau}}-K\delta^{2-}\geq\frac{\acute{\eta}}{2(1+|k|^{\tau})},

since we have
K(1+K|^{\tau})\delta^{2-}\leq\frac{\acute{\eta}}{2}
by the choice of
K
in (
4.64
).

After we perform at most
\log_{2}\mathcal{M}
times iteration, there exists a symplectic transformation such that
\Phi^{*}
the Hamiltonian
H
becomes

H^{*}=H\circ\Phi^{*}=N+Z+B+Q+T,

where

N=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\omega^{*}_{j}(\xi)y_{j}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{[\breve{\Omega}_{j}](\xi)}{j}|z_{j}|^{2}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{\breve{(\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}])(x;\xi)}{j}(|z_{j}|^{2}-|z_{j}(0)|^{2}),

Z=\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 2}Z^{\alpha}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha},\quad B=\sum_{|\beta|\geq 1}B^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)(|z|^{2}-|z(0)|^{2})^{\beta},

Q=\sum_{|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3}Q^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

with the coefficients of
Z
,
Q
,
B
and
T
satisfy the following estimates

\displaystyle\|Z^{\alpha}(x;\xi)-[Z^{\alpha}](\xi)\|_{D(2\varrho)}\leq\delta^{2-},\quad\|B^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)\|_{D(\varrho_{1},\delta_{1})}\leq\delta^{2-},

(4.68)

\displaystyle\|X_{Q}\|_{p/2,D(2\varrho,3\delta,4\delta)},\quad\|X_{T}\|_{p-1,D(2\varrho,3\delta,4\delta)}\leq\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1}.

(4.69)

In addition, the frequency satisfies the small divisor condition

|\langle k,\omega^{*}\rangle|\geq\frac{\acute{\eta}}{2(1+|k|^{\tau})},\quad 0\neq k\in\mathbb{Z}^{n}.

(4.70)

In what follows, we are going to eliminate
Z^{2}(x;\xi)y^{2}
. For this purpose, we write
Z^{2}(x;\xi)y^{2}=\Gamma_{K}Z^{2}(x;\xi)y^{2}+(1-\Gamma_{K})Z^{2}(x;\xi)y^{2}
. Let
\tilde{D}_{j}=\{(x,y):|{\rm Im}\ x|\leq\tilde{\varrho}_{j},|y|\leq\tilde{\delta}_{j}^{2}\}
where
\tilde{\varrho}_{j}=2\varrho-j\varrho/\mathcal{M}
and
\tilde{\delta}_{j}=3\delta-j\delta/\mathcal{M}
.

Let
\Phi=X_{F}^{t}|_{t=1}
be the time-1 map of the vector field
X_{F}
of Hamiltonion

F(x,y;\xi)=F^{2}(x;\xi)y^{2}.

(4.71)

Then, we obtain

\begin{split}H^{*}\circ\Phi=&(N+Z+B+Q+T)\circ\Phi\\
=&N+\{N,F\}+\int_{0}^{1}\{(1-t)\{N,F\}+tZ^{2}(x)y^{2},F\}\circ X_{F}^{t}dt\\
&+\Gamma_{K}Z^{2}(x)y^{2}+(1-\Gamma_{K})Z^{2}(x)y^{2}+((Z-Z^{2}(x)y^{2})+B+Q+T)\circ\Phi.\end{split}

(4.72)

From the above, the homological equation reads,

\{N,F\}=\Gamma_{K}Z^{2}y^{2}-[Z^{2}]y^{2},

where
F
is defined by (
4.71
). By Fourier expansion, in both sides of the homological equation, we have

\sum_{0\neq|k|\leq K}{\bf i}\langle k,\omega^{*}\rangle\widehat{F^{2}_{k}}e^{{\bf i}\langle k,x\rangle}=\sum_{0\neq|k|\leq K}\widehat{Z^{2}_{k}}e^{{\bf i}\langle k,x\rangle}.

Thus, we have

{\bf i}\langle k,\omega^{*}\rangle\widehat{F^{2}_{k}}=\widehat{Z^{2}_{k}}.

According to the small divisor condition (
4.70
),
we have the solution
F(x,y;\xi)=F^{2}(x;\xi)y^{2}
obeys the estimate

\|F^{2}(x;\xi)\|_{\tilde{D}_{1}}\leq\delta^{2-}.

(4.73)

Now let us deal with the remaining terms in (
4.72
). We have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\{(1-t)\{N,F\}+tZ^{2}(x;\xi)y^{2},F\}\circ X_{F}^{t}dt=\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 3}Z^{\alpha}_{+1}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha},

with
\|Z^{\alpha}_{+1}\|_{\tilde{D}_{1}}\leq\delta^{2-}
by (
4.73
) and (
4.68
).

For the term
(1-\Gamma_{K})Z^{2}y^{2}
, we have
\|(1-\Gamma_{K})Z^{2}\|\leq\delta^{\mathcal{M}+2}
.

For the other terms, we have

(Z-Z^{2}(x;\xi)y^{2})\circ\Phi=\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 3}Z_{+2}^{\alpha}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha},

B_{+}=B\circ\Phi=\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 0,|\beta|\geq 1}B_{+}^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)(|z|^{2}-|z(0)|^{2})^{\beta},

Q\circ\Phi=\sum_{|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3}Q^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

with the coefficients of
Z
,
B
,
Q
and
T
satisfy

\|B_{+}^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)\|_{\tilde{D}_{1}},\quad\|Z_{+2}^{\alpha}(x;\xi)-[Z_{+2}^{\alpha}](\xi)\|_{\tilde{D}_{1}}\leq\delta^{2-},

and

\|X_{Q\circ\Phi}\|_{p/2,D(\tilde{\varrho}_{1},\tilde{\delta}_{1},4\delta)},\quad\|X_{T\circ\Phi}\|_{p-1,D(\tilde{\varrho}_{1},\tilde{\delta}_{1},4\delta)}\leq\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1},

respectively.

Performing this process for
\mathcal{M}-2
times, under some transformation
\Phi^{**}
we finally get a Hamiltonian

H^{**}=H^{*}\circ\Phi^{**}=N+Z+B+Q+T,

with

N=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\omega^{*}_{j}(\xi)y_{j}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{[\breve{\Omega}_{j}](\xi)}{j}|z_{j}|^{2}+\sum_{j\geq 1}\frac{\breve{(\Omega}_{j}-[\breve{\Omega}_{j}])(x;\xi)}{j}(|z_{j}|^{2}-|z_{j}(0)|^{2}),

Z=\sum_{|\alpha|\leq\mathcal{M}+2}Z^{\alpha}(\xi)y^{\alpha}+\sum_{|\alpha|\geq\mathcal{M}+3}Z^{\alpha}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha},

(4.74)

B=\sum_{|\beta|\geq 1}B^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)(|z|^{2}-|z(0)|^{2})^{\beta},

Q=\sum_{|\mu|+|\nu|\geq 3}Q^{\alpha\beta\gamma\mu\nu}(x;\xi)y^{\alpha}\acute{z}^{\beta}\bar{\acute{z}}^{\gamma}\hat{z}^{\mu}\bar{\hat{z}}^{\nu},

with the coefficients of
Z
,
B,\ Q
and
T
satisfy

\|Z^{\alpha}(x;\xi)\|_{D(\varrho)}\leq\delta^{2-},\quad|\alpha|\geq\mathcal{M}+3,

(4.75)

\|B^{\beta}(x,y;\xi)\|_{D(\varrho,2\delta)}\leq\delta^{2-},\quad|\beta|\geq 1,

(4.76)

and

\|X_{Q}\|_{p/2,D(\varrho,2\delta,4\delta)},\quad\|X_{T}\|_{p-1,D(\varrho,2\delta,4\delta)}\leq\delta^{\mathcal{M}+1},

(4.77)

respectively.

Having the previous preparation, we are now in a position to do the long time stability estimate for the KAM tori obtained in Remark
2.7
.

Assume
w(t)=(x(t),y(t),z(t),\overline{z}(t))
is the solution of Hamiltonian vector field
X_{H^{**}}(x,y,z,\overline{z})
with the initial datum

w(0)=(x(0),y(0),z(0),\bar{z}(0))\in D(\varrho,2\delta,4\delta)

satisfying

d_{p}(w(0),\mathcal{T}_{0})\leq\delta.

Noting
\mathcal{T}_{0}=\mathbb{T}^{n}\times\{y=0\}\times\{z=0\}\times\{\bar{z}=0\}
, we have

\frac{1}{4\delta}||y(0)||+||z(0)||_{p}+||{\bar{z}}(0)||_{p}\leq\delta.

The flow on the torus
\mathcal{T}_{0}
is
x_{0}(t)=x_{0}+\breve{\omega}t
. Now, we will estimate
||z(t)||_{p/2}
and
||{\bar{z}}(t)||_{p/2}
. For this purpose, we let

\tilde{N}(z,\bar{z}):=||z||_{p/2}^{2}=\sum_{j\geq 1}|z_{j}|^{2}|j|^{2p/2}.

By initial data, we have

\tilde{N}(w(0))\leq\delta^{2}.
